{\n357\t\treturn ch == '\\n' || ch == -1\n358\t}\n359\t\n360\t// isBlank returns true if the rune is empty character..\n361\tfunc isBlank(ch rune) bool {\n362\t\treturn ch == ' ' || ch == '\\t' || ch == '\\r'\n363\t}\n364\t\n365\t// peek returns current rune in the code.\n366\tfunc (s *Scanner) peek() rune {\n367\t\tif s.reachEOF() {\n368\t\t\treturn EOF\n369\t\t}\n370\t\treturn beginning at current position.\n438\tfunc (s *Scanner) scanNumber() (string, error) {\n439\t\tresult := []rune{s.peek()}\n440\t\ts.next()\n441\t\n442\t\tif result[0] == '0' && (s.peek() == 'x' || ret []rune\n522\teos:\n523\t\tfor {\n524\t\t\ts.next()\n525\t\t\tswitch s.peek() {\n526\t\t\tcase EOL:\n527\t\t\t\treturn \"\", errors.New(\"unexpected EOL\")\n528\t\t\tcase EOF:\n529\t\t\t\treturn string(ret), nil\n559\t}\n560\t\n561\t// Lexer provides interface to parse codes.\n562\ttype Lexer struct {\n563\t\ts    *Scanner\n564\t\tlit  string\n565\t\tpos  ast.Position\n566\t\te    error\n567\t\tstmt ast.Stmt\n568\t}\n569\t\n570\t// Lex scans the token and literals.\n571\tfunc (l *Lexer) Lex(lval *yySymType) int {\n572\t\ttok, lit, pos, err := l.s.Scan()\n573\t\tif err != nil toNumber(numString string) (reflect.Value, error) {\n616\t\t// hex\n617\t\tif len(numString) > 2 && numString[0:2] == \"0x\" {\n618\t\t\ti, err := strconv.ParseInt(numString[2:], 16, 64)\n619\t\t\tif err != nil {\n620\t\t\t\treturn nilValue, err\n621\t\t\t}\n622\t\t\treturn reflect.ValueOf(i), nil\n623\t\t}\n624\t\n625\t\t// hex\n626\t\tif len(numString) > 3 && numString[0:3] == \"-0x\" {\n627\t\t\ti, err := strconv.ParseInt(\"-\"+numString[3:], 16, 64)\n628\t\t\tif err != nil {\n629\t\t\t\treturn nilValue, err\n630\t\t\t}\n631\t\t\treturn reflect.ValueOf(i), true\n425\t}\n426\t\n427\t// processCallReturnValues get/converts the values returned from a function call into our normal reflect.Value, error\n428\tfunc processCallReturnValues(rvs []reflect.Value, isRunVMFunction bool, convertToInterfaceSlice bool) (reflect.Value, error) {\n429\t\t// check if it is not runVMFunction\n430\t\tif !isRunVMFunction {\n431\t\t\t// the function was a Go function, convert to our normal reflect.Value, error\n432\t\t\tswitch len(rvs) {\n433\t\t\tcase 0:\n434\t\t\t\t// no return values so return nil reflect.Value and nil error\n435\t\t\t\treturn nilValue, nil\n436\t\t\tcase 1:\n437\t\t\t\t// one return value but need to add nil error\n438\t\t\t\treturn rvs[0], nil\n439\t\t\t}\n440\t\t\tif convertToInterfaceSlice {\n441\t\t\t\t// need to convert from a slice of reflect.Value to slice of interface\n442\t\t\t\treturn reflectValueSlicetoInterfaceSlice(rvs), nil\n443\t\t\t}\n444\t\t\t// need to keep as slice of reflect.Value\n445\t\t\treturn reflect.ValueOf(rvs), nil\n446\t\t}\n447\t\n448\t\t// is a runVMFunction, expect return in the runVMFunction format\n449\t\t// convertToInterfaceSlice is ignored\n450\t\t// some of the below checks probably can be removed because they are done in checkIfRunVMFunction\n451\t\n452\t\tif len(rvs) != 2 {\n453\t\t\treturn nilValue, fmt.Errorf(\"VM function did not return 2 values but returned %v values\", len(rvs))\n454\t\t}\n455\t\tif rvs[0].Type() != reflectValueType {\n456\t\t\treturn nilValue, fmt.Errorf(\"VM function value 1 did not return reflect value type but returned %v type\",
